Getting Started

1-Week Course - Getting Started with GenFarmer BoxPhone

The one-week course plus the full setup and software guide, in one place. Version 1.1 · 09/2026.

Five days to master the system, not five days to see results. Results take 2-4 weeks of warming up accounts. These are two different milestones, and knowing that up front saves you disappointment.

Watch the video tutorial Contact support

How this documentation is organised. The one-week course teaches the order of things and the reason behind each one. Setup and Software hold the step-by-step procedures with screenshots, so the course can stay short and never repeat them. Reference is what you open once something is already wrong.

How the pieces fit together

The whole system in one picture: the box, the router, the control app and the automation package - what each one does, and in what order you put them together. Each piece is taught in full on its own page, listed in the table below.

Layer 1Boxphone20 phones per box
Layer 2RouterOne network route per phone
ThroughoutControl appControl and manage
Layer 4PackageRuns the work for you
Boxphone
Hardware · Usually 20 phones per box
  • 1 box + GenRouter H3000 goes in USB mode
  • Mini PC goes in OTG mode: add the two address ranges, click Active, then Scan
  • Done when Scan finds 20/20 phones and a phone responds when you toggle the flashlight
Router
GenRouter H3000 (1 box) · Mini PC (from 2 boxes)
  • Each phone gets its own internet route, nothing shared
  • Assign one proxy per phone
  • Isolate Mode: when a proxy dies, that phone's network is cut immediately
  • Control panel at 192.168.5.1:9000
Control app
GenFarmer software · Installed on the control computer · The hub of the system
  • Control Center: control all 20 phones, synchronized actions, install APKs
  • Account Manager: import the account sheet, assign Device IDs, choose which account runs which script
  • Store: download the automation package
Package
Ready-made scripts · TikTok, Facebook, Instagram, X, Spotify
  • Auto Login: log accounts in and out in bulk
  • Trust: warm up accounts, run for 2-4 weeks
  • Boost: push engagement, only after enough warming up
  • If you have not bought one yet, contact GenFarmer sales on WhatsApp +84 97 123 46 01

Where is layer 3? Account + Proxy has no box of its own in this picture because it does not sit at one place in the chain: the proxy is assigned in the router, and the account sheet lives in the control app. You do both on Day 2.

Control appDay 1 onwards, every dayDay 1 · Day 2

PreviousContents NextWhat is Boxphone?

Last updated 09/2026

Getting Started

What is Boxphone?

The hardware itself: what is inside the box, what people run on it, and why a box beats a shelf of loose phones.

Boxphone (short for Box Phone Farm) is a hardware system that holds and manages dozens to hundreds of smartphone mainboards inside one compact box. Instead of many separate phones, the core components are consolidated into a single box to run automated tasks on social platforms.

Features and structure

  • Optimized design: smartphone mainboards - usually with screens and batteries removed to prevent fire hazards - are stacked or arranged side by side inside a metal enclosure
  • Centralized connection: every device in the box connects to the computer through a single cable and draws stable power
  • Remote control: one piece of software on the computer drives all the phones at once

What people use it for

The system is used for marketing and making money online:

  • Artificial engagement growth: likes, shares, comments or video views on Facebook, TikTok and YouTube to create a crowd effect
  • Account farming: managing a large number of accounts and simulating real user behaviour to avoid bans
  • Advertising and bulk messaging: sending messages or posting content in groups
  • Game and app farming: automating tasks to earn rewards

Why a box beats loose phones

  • Space saving: far more compact than hundreds of complete handsets
  • Higher durability: with the batteries removed - the main fire risk during continuous charging - and the screens gone, the system runs cooler and more stably
  • Centralized management: everything is monitored and maintained from one software interface

Where the box sits in the system as a whole is on the front page.

PreviousThe four layers of the system NextWhat is Change / SuperChange?

Last updated 09/2026

Getting Started

What is Change / SuperChange?

GenFarmer's own technology: how one device is made to look like many, and what Super Change adds on top.

What is Change?

Change modifies and emulates Android device fingerprints at ROM and hardware level, so an automation device looks like a real user's phone. In one line: Change turns one device into several distinct devices in terms of system identity. It is pre-installed in the ROM of certain Boxphone models and works only inside the GenFarmer application.

  • ADB hiding: ADB is the channel used to control Android devices remotely. When platforms such as Instagram, TikTok or X detect it, the result is reduced reach, shadowbans or suspension. S8 Change hides ADB so the device looks like an ordinary phone.
  • Upgraded Android ROM: the original Galaxy S8 only supports Android 9, which is old enough to be flagged. S8 Change upgrades the ROM to Android 14, and can go back to Android 12-13 when needed.
  • Diverse fingerprints: identical models, MAC addresses or fingerprints across a farm form a detectable pattern. S8 Change can emulate brands such as Oppo, Xiaomi, Huawei or Vivo instead.
  • Restore and backup: account data such as login state survives a device swap, a reset or a transfer - see Change / Backup / Restore.

What is SuperChange?

Some machines are built to run smoothly for years, others degrade fast, and the difference is the engineering inside rather than the price tag. Super Change is the well-engineered kind: built and optimized at the factory to run smoothly and stay hard to detect.

  1. Change / Backup / Restore - each device gets a new identity plus a backup copy that restores in minutes without interrupting operations.
  2. GPS - location simulation - platforms watch location and movement, so Super Change picks realistic places and simulates natural movement through the day.
  3. Sensor simulation - real phones get picked up and tilted; a perfectly still device stands out, so sensor activity is simulated.
  4. Official app installation sources - apps install as if from the official stores, which reduces errors and feature restrictions, with no need to sign in to Gmail on every device.
  5. ADB hiding - automation still needs a control channel, but the channel stays hidden.
  6. Custom ROM, Android 15, OTA updates - a purpose-built ROM, updated remotely without shipping devices back.
  7. Strong hardware and rare fingerprints - modern, high-spec models rarely seen in phone farms, mostly Snapdragon 835 with 6-8GB RAM and 128GB storage, so the fingerprints are hard to cluster.
Super Change Review: Android 15, 100% Hidden ADB & Real Sensor Emulation

What it changes in practice

  • Fewer checkpoints and fewer bans, most noticeably at scale
  • Scales from dozens to thousands of devices without losing safety
  • Each device behaves like a separate real user
  • Remote updates and fixes instead of downtime

Change is not root. Change modifies how applications recognise the device; root modifies how the operating system works. Change adjusts device parameters at the surface, which is usually enough to pass platform checks, while root goes deep into the system and is easier for Google or YouTube to detect.

RoadmapOne-week roadmap

Day 0 - Preparation

Do this before the shipment arrives. No need to plug anything in yet - just have everything below ready before you start Day 1.

Do this before the shipment arrives. The first three days are the most exciting; do not let them slip by while you wait to buy proxies.

Question of the day: What do I need to have ready?

No need to plug anything in yet. Just have every item below ready before you start Day 1.

A place for the box + a dedicated power supply → Wired network + a control computer → Accounts and trial proxies bought in advance → An unboxing video

Preparation

  • A place for the box - a well-ventilated or air-conditioned room, about 10 cm away from the wall so air can flow on all four sides. Do not put it on the floor, inside a closed cabinet, or next to a sunny window.
  • A dedicated power supply - do not share a socket with an air conditioner, oven or washing machine. A UPS of 1,000 VA or more is recommended so the system does not shut down abruptly during a power cut. Have a 3-prong socket ready if you do not have one. Check the voltage your box takes: Box P30 runs on 100-240V, P30+ on 180-240V.
  • A wired network connection - one free port on your modem for the GenRouter. From around 4 boxes up you will also want a LAN switch between the mini PC and the boxes; the documented stable cluster is 1 PC + 1 mini PC + 1 switch + 10 boxes. Details: Router & proxy.
  • LAN cable (if you use a mini PC) - Cat5 or Cat6 (preferred).
  • A control computer - minimum Windows 10 64-bit with a dedicated GPU, at least an NVIDIA GeForce GTX 1050. Wi-Fi is not required; a LAN connection works just as well. See the table below for how to connect it, and Requirements and safety notes for the full specification.
  • 5 accounts and 5 proxies for a trial run - buy them in advance, do not wait until you need them. You do not need 20 accounts yet.
  • vilao.ai API key - used for automatic commenting. By default the script uses its built-in model; you can switch to another AI model available on vilao if you need to. The fee is about 2% ≈ 50,000 VND, enough for generous use. Sign up and get your key at vilao.ai/console - step-by-step guide.
  • Unboxing video - film yourself opening the package, and keep the seals intact. This is your warranty evidence in case of a manufacturing defect.

The exact specification behind this list - minimum PC configuration, the voltage each box model takes, cable standards and the two rules that void the warranty - is on Requirements and safety notes.

How to connect the control computer

You useHow to connect
GenRouter H3000If your PC has Wi-Fi, connect over Wi-Fi. If not, use LAN: H3000 LAN port → PC Ethernet, or Boxphone LAN → PC Ethernet (in this case connect the H3000 LAN port to LAN1 or LAN2 of the Boxphone, then run the remaining LAN port to the PC - applies to the P30 box).
Mini PCConnect over LAN. Wi-Fi does not apply to the mini PC. If your laptop has no Ethernet port, get a USB-to-LAN or Type-C-to-LAN adapter.

In the User Manual (HDSD) (p. 30-39), the API key section still says "Apple Key" / Gemini - that is the old version. The system now uses the vilao.ai API; follow the instructions on this page instead.

Before you touch a single phone: in the device settings of the Boxphone, never enable Developer Mode or USB Debugging. It can turn the screen off permanently and leave the device unusable, and hardware altered without guidance falls outside the warranty.

Done when

You have every item above. It is fine if nothing is plugged in yet.

Four common mistakes

✕MistakeConsequence
1Putting the box in a closed cabinet or under a desk to keep things tidyHeat cannot escape, the phones throttle themselves
2Sharing a power socket with high-power appliancesThe system crashes in the middle of a run
3Buying a mini PC without a LAN cable, or using a laptop with no Ethernet port and no adapterThe shipment arrives and you still cannot connect your computer
4Enabling Developer Mode or USB Debugging on the phonesThe screen can switch off for good, the device becomes unusable, and altered hardware is outside the warranty

RoadmapOne-week roadmap

Day 1 - Mastering the hardware

Layer 1. Use the GenFarmer software at a basic level to get a feel for how it works. Done when 20/20 phones show up and the phone responds when you toggle the flashlight.

Layer 1 · Boxphone

Question of the day: Do the phones obey me?

Day 1: use the GenFarmer software at a basic level to get a feel for how it works.

Download the software → Set up the box + router → Use the software manually at a basic level

Steps

  1. Download the software

    Download the GenFarmer software from genfarmer.com/download and install it. User Manual (HDSD) p. 1

  2. Log in

    Click the avatar icon and choose Login. If you do not have an account yet, register first. HDSD p. 2-3

  3. Choose the connection mode that matches your equipment

    This is where most people get stuck, so read carefully.

    You haveModeWhy
    1 box + GenRouter H3000USBThe H3000 can only drive 1 box; in practice it cannot handle 2
    Mini PC (even with just 1-2 boxes)OTGThe phones use the mini PC's network, so OTG is the default
    No mini PC, no H3000OTG or Wi-FiFlexible, depending on your needs
    Connect the Boxphone via USB
    Four steps, with the light code that tells you which mode the box is in.
    Connect the Boxphone via OTG (LAN)
    Address ranges, Activate, the two presses on the power button, then Scan.

    The two pages above carry the full procedure with screenshots, so it is not repeated here. What matters on Day 1 is picking the right row in the table and finishing it.

    The HDSD shows three wiring diagrams side by side. Pick the one that matches the table above; you do not need to do all three.

  4. Use the GenFarmer software manually at a basic level

    The Control Center section. HDSD p. 4-7

  5. Turn on Wi-Fi automatically on all 20 phones

    The procedure is in this walkthrough; the same video also covers the system time on the next step.

    How to set language, timezone and Wi-Fi via the GenFarmer app

  6. Change the system time on the phones

    Match it to the time zone you are using - you can match it to the time on your PC.

    Shown in the same walkthrough as the previous step, How to set language, timezone and Wi-Fi via the GenFarmer app.

  7. Toggle the flashlight on any one phone

    This is the smallest yet clearest test: if the phone responds within a few seconds, the control path is working.

    See the procedure in Control devices individually and in sync, or the full walkthrough on Synchronized control and APK installation.

  8. Try synchronized actions

    Tap on one phone and watch all 20 phones follow.

    See the procedure in Control devices individually and in sync, or the full walkthrough on Synchronized control and APK installation.

  9. Spend 15 minutes tapping around on a few phones by hand

    This step looks like a waste of time, but it is not - Day 3 relies entirely on this feel.

  10. Optional: try a few adb commands

    If you have a technical background. Skipping this changes nothing.

Done when

20 out of 20 phones appear on screen, all of them are connected to Wi-Fi, and the phone responds when you toggle the flashlight.

If you are not there yet, stop here and do not move on to Day 2. Contact support with this information: how many boxes you use, USB or OTG mode, and how many phones Scan finds.

Three common mistakes

✕MistakeCause / fix
1Scan finds no phonesAlmost always because both address ranges were not added, or they were added but Active was never clicked
2OTG is plugged in but the phone has no internetCheck the proxy first; if the proxy is fine, check the time on the phone
3Using an H3000 for 2 boxesThe H3000 can only drive 1 box. From 2 boxes up you need a mini PC and OTG mode

The control computer does not need Wi-Fi - you can turn Wi-Fi off and connect over LAN; it works just the same (for both the H3000 and the mini PC). Wi-Fi does not apply to the mini PC.

RoadmapOne-week roadmap

Day 2 - Mastering identity

Layers 2 + 3. The most important day of the week. You only touch 5 accounts, and most of the time is spent waiting.

Layer 2 · Router + Layer 3 · Account + Proxy

The most important day of the week - and the part the User Manual (HDSD) does not cover in depth. Read this page carefully.

Question of the day: Will my accounts survive?

Start small, start slow. Today you only touch 5 accounts, and most of the time is spent waiting.

Why accounts get banned in clusters

An account is an identity. A proxy is that identity's home address.

If 20 accounts share one address, the platform sees 20 people living in the same house, waking and sleeping at the same time, liking the same kind of content. That is why accounts get banned in clusters rather than one by one.

GenRouter exists so that each phone has its own address. Isolate Mode does one more thing: if a proxy dies, it cuts that phone's network immediately instead of letting the phone expose your real address.

✕ Wrong - 20 accounts, 1 address

1 proxy → Acct 1Acct 2… Acct 20

✓ Right - one address per phone

proxy 1→Acct 1
proxy 2→Acct 2
proxy …→Acct …
Prepare accounts + proxies → Assign proxies + Isolate Mode → Install the platform app → Log in by hand as a test → Import the account sheet → Run a simple job so the accounts get warmed up

Steps

  1. Prepare 5 accounts and 5 proxies

    If you want to save money for the trial, 5 accounts spread over 2-3 proxies is acceptable, but remember that this is not a real production setup.

  2. Assign a proxy to each phone and turn on Isolate Mode

    Open the GenRouter control panel at 192.168.5.1:9000, assign a proxy to each phone, and turn on Isolate Mode.

    Choosing and buying proxies, and what to do when the network drops: Router & proxy →

    How to use GenRouter: assign a proxy per device

    If it still does not work after following the video below, contact support before you log in to any account.

  3. Install the platform app on the phones

    Download the APK file, select all phones, and click Install APK. Step by step with screenshots: Synchronized control and APK installation. HDSD p. 17 · 51 · 78 · 102 · 124

    Install it on every phone today: Day 2 is when you start logging accounts in gradually to let them settle, so installing in advance saves work on the following days.

    Do not download the APK from the links in the HDSD. The APK links for Facebook, Instagram, X and Spotify in the HDSD are currently wrong (they point to the TikTok file). Download the APKs from the official folder below.

    Official APK folder (includes TikTok, Facebook, Instagram, X, Spotify): APK_GENFARMER - Google Drive

  4. Log in to 1-2 accounts by hand

    Go slowly and watch every step.

    This is the most important step of the whole week: the automation later only repeats exactly what you have just done by hand. If an account is already asked for verification when you do it by hand, the automation will hit the same thing, just 20 times faster.

  5. Create an account sheet in Account Manager

    Only do this once the 1-2 accounts from the previous step are stable. Every field and button on that screen is described in Account Manager. HDSD p. 10-11

  6. Enter all 5 accounts into the sheet

    The default format is UID|Password|2FA; to change it, go to Custom. HDSD p. 11-13

  7. Assign a device to each account

    Copy the Device ID from the main screen and paste it into the Device ID column. HDSD p. 14-15

  8. Leave everything alone for 48 hours

    Do not run anything. This is not dead time - this is the test.

Done when

5 out of 5 accounts can still log in after 48 hours, and none of them has been asked for verification or temporarily locked.

If you are not there yet, do not buy more accounts under any circumstances. Change your account supplier or change proxies, then retry from step 4 (log in by hand). Buying 20 accounts while 5 are still not surviving is the fastest way to waste money.

Three common mistakes

✕MistakeConsequence
1Buying 20 accounts right away to fill the whole setYou lose all 20 in a single night
2Sharing one proxy across many accounts to save moneyAccounts get banned in clusters, and you wrongly conclude the hardware is at fault
3Logging in to many accounts on the same phone one after another in a short timeNo real person does that

RoadmapOne-week roadmap

Day 3 - Basic automation

Layer 4. Today you do not learn a new feature - you simply hand over to the machine the things you did by hand over the past two days.

Layer 4 · Automation

Question of the day: Can the machine do the work for me?

Today you do not learn a new feature. You simply hand over to the machine the things you did by hand over the past two days.

What automation is

It is the very same actions you tapped by hand on Day 1 and Day 2, recorded as a script and run on many phones at once. No coding needed; you just select and click.

ToolWhat it doesWhen to use it
Auto LoginLogs in and logs out in bulk instead of typing on each phone by handToday
Trust (warming up accounts)The phone watches videos, scrolls, likes and follows like a real person with clear interests. The goal is for the platform to register this account as belonging to someone with habits, not a brand-new account.Run for 2-4 weeks
Boost (boosting engagement)Concentrates engagement on one specific post so the platform distributes it more widelyOnly after enough warming up

Package by package, with the figures to run each one by: Automation packages →

Turning on Boost on an account that has not been warmed up is the fastest way to lose it, and an account that has once run junk engagement is very hard to recover.

Download the automation package → Run Auto Login → Turn on Trust to warm up accounts

Steps

  1. Check that the platform app is on the phones

    The platform app was installed on Day 2 · step 3. Install it on any phone that is missing it, following the instructions there - download from the official APK folder, not the links in the User Manual (HDSD).

  2. Download the automation package for your platform

    Go to the Store - see App Manager for the Store itself and Automation for how Apps, Tasks and Runs fit together - find your package and download it. A video guide is included - see the GenFarmer video playlist. HDSD p. 18-20 · 52-55 · 80-83

  3. Download the platform's three tools

    Auto Login, Trust and Boost. HDSD p. 21-24 · 56-59 · 84-88

  4. Open the Account Manager screen

    This is where you choose which account runs which script. On Day 2 you used this screen to store accounts; today you use it to give orders.

  5. Run Auto Login on exactly the 5 accounts you already have

    Do not scale up yet.

  6. Turn on Trust for those 5 accounts

    Let it run overnight.

  7. Check the next morning

    If all 5 are still alive, buy more to reach 20, then run Auto Login and Trust on all 20 phones.

Done when

20 phones log in on their own and warm up accounts overnight without you having to step in.

If any account gets banned overnight, do not scale up. Go back and check the proxies and the running speed first.

Three common mistakes

✕MistakeConsequence
1Turning on Boost on the first day because you want to see results earlyThe most expensive mistake in the whole roadmap
2Running at full capacity on the first run instead of ramping up graduallyAccounts get banned in bulk
3Only discovering you have no vilao.ai API key when you reach automatic commentingYou have to stop halfway. This was already part of Day 0. Note: even with the right API, if the provider's model has an outage, automatic commenting may still be affected

Question of the day: What can it produce for me?

These two days are for becoming fluent, not for learning new concepts.

Read your platform chapter → Configure for your content niche → Run a test script → Learn the signs that mean stop

Steps

  1. Read your platform chapter

    Focus on the configuration section.

  2. Configure it for your actual content niche

    Pick the wrong topic and your accounts will be warmed up as people interested in something else, and your posts will not reach the right audience.

    Recommended settings for the package you are running: Automation packages →

  3. Run one complete script from start to finish

    Read the results yourself.

  4. Learn to recognize the three signs that mean stop

    1. Accounts are asked for verification more often than usual.
    2. Posts suddenly lose all their views.
    3. The number of running phones keeps dropping for no clear reason.

Done when

You can configure a new script yourself without opening the documentation again.

From here on you are in the warming-up phase, which lasts 2-4 weeks. During that time the numbers grow slowly, and that is normal. Do not speed up to compensate.

Last updated 09/2026

Setup

Requirements and safety notes

What your computer, your power supply and your cables have to be before the first box is plugged in - plus the two rules that void the warranty.

Everything on this page comes before you plug anything in. The checklist of what to buy is on Day 0; this page is the hardware and safety detail behind that checklist.

Minimum PC configuration (controller computer)

  • Operating system: Windows 10, 64-bit
  • GPU: a dedicated GPU is required, minimum NVIDIA GeForce GTX 1050

GenFarmer is built for Windows and works best on Windows 10. Use that version for the smoothest experience.

Power supply

Boxphone modelSupported voltage
Box P30100V - 240V
Box P30+180V - 240V

Use a stable power source with enough capacity for the number of Boxphones you run. This prevents the overloads and voltage drops that make devices misbehave.

Safety rules

In the device settings of the Boxphone, never enable Developer Mode or USB Debugging. Doing so can turn the screen off permanently and leave the device unusable.

Do not modify or interfere with the hardware of the Boxphone without guidance from the technical team. Products with altered hardware or software fall outside the warranty and return policy.

Network and identity

How to set up your router for maximum performance

To run many accounts safely, a proxy or VPN is essential, and the recommended setup is a separate IP address for each device. GenFarmer sells the H3000 router and the MiniPC for exactly this. The reason behind the rule is on Day 2.

Cables

  • If you are not using GenFarmer cables, pick USB cables from a known brand such as Ugreen
  • For LAN, use Cat-6 cable to keep transmission stable

ADB environment

How to set up ADB on Windows

Setting up the ADB environment on Windows lets you control the Boxphone and diagnose problems yourself. It is also what the Unauthorized device fix depends on, so it is worth doing before you need it. See ADB commands.

Last updated 09/2026

Setup

Buttons, ports and lights

Every physical control on the box and the router, the light codes, and the buttons in the app that drive the same things.

One page for every physical control on the hardware and every button in the app that drives it. If you are looking for where do I press to do this, start here rather than reading a procedure end to end.

Port and LED light guide

On the Boxphone

The power button

What you doWhat happens
Press onceThe box turns on. The fans start and the light comes up
Press twiceThe box switches connection mode, between USB and OTG

That second press is the one people miss. In OTG mode you press it right after the devices disconnect to release the port, and in USB mode you press it if the light came up green when you wanted blue.

The indicator light

LightMeaningWhat to do
BlueThe box is in USB modeCarry on - this is what you want with a GenRouter H3000
GreenThe box is in OTG modeThis is what you want with a mini PC
Red, or flickeringA mainboard fault, and the device drops out of the softwareFix: indicator light error - swap the mainboard to another slot, then reseat the FPC cable

The ports

  • USB and LAN/OTG on the same box. You switch between them with that one button, and GenFarmer does not charge to change the port
  • Boxphones with a LAN port can be chained to each other and run in OTG - see Router & proxy
  • Cooling is 4 fans, and the box runs on a 550W Turbo power supply

In the device settings of the phones, never enable Developer Mode or USB Debugging. It can turn the screen off permanently and leave the device unusable, and hardware altered without guidance falls outside the warranty.

On the router

DeviceControls
GenRouter H3000Power it on and it lights up green. Modem into WAN, PC into LAN, or join the GENROUTER-2.4GHz / GENROUTER-5GHz network. The control panel is at 192.168.5.1:9000
Mini PCModem into ETH0, PC into ETH1, ETH2 or ETH3, boxes into whatever is left. Same control panel at 192.168.5.1:9000

Never press the physical reset button on the router unless technical support tells you to, and do not change the device IP on your computer by hand. Both are quick ways to lose a working setup.

In the app: the hardware you can drive remotely

You do not have to be standing next to the box for most of it.

  • Fan speed can be adjusted from the software
  • LAN and USB can be switched remotely, without touching the button
  • Real-time monitoring watches each device and resets it automatically if it drops the connection

In the app: the stream toolbar

Double-click a device to open its stream, and this is the row of controls you get - the full screen is described in Control Center.

ButtonWhat it does
Vol+ / Vol-Volume up and down
ScreenshotCapture the device screen
PowerTurn the screen off
RotateRotate the screen
Install APKInstall an app straight from the stream
ADB CommandRun an ADB command on that device
RebootRestart the device
Switch Keyboard MethodOpen the keyboard settings
Accessibility SettingsOpen the settings interface
LanguageOpen the language settings
Connection SettingWi-Fi, Bluetooth and the rest
AppsList the apps installed on that device

Quick lookup: I want to do this, where do I press

What you wantWhere
Turn the box onPower button on the box, once
Switch between USB and OTGPower button on the box, twice - or from the software
Check which mode the box is inThe light: blue is USB, green is OTG
Control one phoneDouble-click it in Control Center
Control twenty phones at onceSelect all, then drive one of them - Synchronized control
Install an app everywhereSelect the devices, right-click, Install APK - Synchronized control
Store accounts and assign devicesAccount Manager
Give each phone its own address192.168.5.1:9000 - Router & proxy
Download a packageThe Store - App Manager
Run a package on a scheduleTasks and Runs - Automation
Change what an app sees as the deviceChange device - Change / Backup / Restore
Change the language or the storage pathSettings

Last updated 09/2026

Setup

Connect the Boxphone via USB

For 1 box with a GenRouter H3000. Four steps, and a light that tells you which mode you are in.

Use this mode when you have 1 box + a GenRouter H3000. The H3000 drives one box only; from two boxes up you need a mini PC and OTG mode.

Video guide: Connect the Boxphone via USB

Port and LED light guide

Steps

  1. Connect the Boxphone to the power supply

  2. Power on the Boxphone

    Press the power button once to turn the Boxphone on. The fan starts running and a blue light means the device is in USB mode.

    If the light is green, press the power button twice to switch modes.

  3. Connect the USB cable

    Connect the USB cable from the Boxphone to the controller computer.

  4. Open the GenFarmer app

    Launch the GenFarmer application to start using the system.

The light code is worth memorising: blue = USB mode, green = OTG mode, red or flickering = a mainboard problem (see the fix).

Next, learn to control the phones: Synchronized control and APK installation.

PreviousButtons, ports and lights NextConnect the Boxphone via OTG (LAN)

Last updated 09/2026

Setup

Connect the Boxphone via OTG (LAN)

For a mini PC, and the only workable mode from 2 boxes up. Both halves of the procedure, hardware and software, in one list.

OTG stands for On-The-Go: connecting the devices to the PC directly over the LAN. This is the default mode when you use a mini PC, and the only workable mode from 2 boxes up.

Video guide: How to set up OTG with GenFarmer

Before you start

  • The PC and the Boxphone must be on the same modem or Wi-Fi router
  • Plug in both the USB and the LAN cable to the computer or router
How to set up OTG with GenFarmer

Steps

  1. Open the OTG section in the GenFarmer control panel

  2. Add the two address ranges

    Add 192.168.4.1-255 and 192.168.5.1-255 so the software knows where to look for the devices.

  3. Select the devices and click Activate

    Select every device you want to switch to OTG, then click Activate.

  4. Press the power button twice on the Boxphone

    As soon as the devices disconnect to release the port, press the power button twice on the Boxphone to switch it into OTG mode.

  5. Wait until every light is green

    Green on all lights means every mainboard is in OTG mode.

  6. Click Scan

    Go back to the GenFarmer software and click Scan to detect the devices.

In OTG mode the phones already have internet access through the mini PC, so you do not need to set up Wi-Fi on them.

Scan finds no phones - almost always because the two address ranges were not both added, or they were added but Activate was never clicked. Check those two before anything else.

OTG is plugged in but a phone still has no internet. Check in this order: first the proxy, and if the proxy is fine, the time on the phone - correct it to match the time on your PC.

Next: Synchronized control and APK installation.

Setup

Synchronized control and APK installation

Driving one phone, driving twenty at once, and installing an app on all of them.

This is the day-to-day skill: driving one phone, driving twenty at once, and putting an app on all of them.

Video guide: Control devices individually and in sync, install APKs

Control devices individually and in sync, install APKs

Individual control

Double-click the device you want to control and operate it directly.

Synchronized control

Click Select All Devices, press Ctrl + A, or drag to select several devices. Then double-click any one of them to control it - every other selected device repeats the same actions.

To leave one device out while Select all is on, hold Ctrl and click that device.

Installing an APK

  1. Get the APK file

    Download it from a source such as APKPure or APKCombo. For the platform apps used in the roadmap, use the official GenFarmer folder listed in Day 2, not the links printed in the User Manual.

  2. Select the devices and choose Install APK

    Select all of them, or only the ones you want, right-click and choose Install APK.

  3. Pick the file and install

    Choose the APK file on your computer, then click Install.

  4. Wait for the green light

    Installation time depends on the size of the app. The indicator turns green when it has finished.

Match the app version to the Android version of the device, otherwise the install fails or the app crashes on launch. The versions available inside GenFarmer are listed in App Manager.

ADB commands

Optional. Run a command across the devices from inside GenFarmer, and set up the ADB environment the Unauthorized fix depends on.

How to use ADB commands in GenFarmer

Running a command

Select ADB Command in the toolbar.

Paste your command into the input field and click Execute.

You can have an AI tool such as ChatGPT or Gemini write the command for you. For example, to open Chrome everywhere, ask for "ADB command to open Google Chrome on Android devices", then paste the result into the field and run it.

PreviousSynchronized control and APK installation NextChange / Backup / Restore

Last updated 09/2026

Setup

Change / Backup / Restore

Give each app its own device identity, and keep account data that survives a reset or a swap.

Change gives a device a new identity for each application. Backup and Restore keep the account data of an app so you do not have to log in again. The technology behind it is explained in What is Change / SuperChange.

Change Device - Back Up - Restore in GenFarmer

Change device

This feature lets you modify the device information seen by each application. In practice you can run two Telegram apps on the same phone and have each app see a different device.

  1. Select the device and open Change device

    Select the device, then click Change device in the side menu. The default identity is Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra 5G.

  2. Randomise or set the parameters by hand

    Click Random to let the system randomise every parameter. To set them yourself, tick Manual in the top-right corner and adjust as you like.

  3. Confirm

    Click Change. The device disconnects and reboots briefly to apply the new settings.

  4. Verify

    Open the Device Info HW app to check the new identity - after the change the device may show up as Realme RMX2030. The app is a free download from APKPure.

Backup: social media account data

  1. Open the account you want to back up

    Go to the platform, open the account profile and check that everything is as it should be.

  2. Back up the app

    Close the app, hover over it (TikTok, for example) and select Backup App.

Restore: social media account data

  1. Choose Restore App

    With the backup file saved, return to the app and select Restore App.

  2. Pick the backup file

    Choose the file you saved earlier.

  3. Reopen the app

    Once you see Restore successful, close the app and open it again to carry on using it.

Backup and Restore are what make a swap painless: when you move an account to another device or reset one, you restore instead of logging in again and going through verification.

PreviousADB commands NextControl Center

Last updated 09/2026

Software

Control Center

The screen you use every day: the sidebar, the stream view, synchronized control and the Inspector.

The Control Center is where you spend most of your time: the grid of phones, the toolbar that tunes how they are displayed, the stream view of a single device, synchronized control, and the Inspector for anyone writing scripts.

Sidebar menu

The toolbar on the left of the control panel holds the functions for managing devices and adjusting how they look on screen.

Adjust device quality

  • Big Screen: the size of a device's stream window. Minimum 40%, maximum 100%
  • Small Screen: the size of the device tiles inside Control Devices - Thumbnail, Medium, Large or Extra Large
  • Big Quality: the image quality of the stream - Low, Medium, High or Extra (Extra gives the best experience)
  • Small Quality: the image quality of the tiles on the overview screen - Low, Medium, High or Extra

Device management

  • Select all: select every device to install APKs, stop tasks and so on
  • Select device: select individual devices to work on

To deselect one device while Select all is active, hold Ctrl and click it.

Stream Device

Stream Device shows a phone screen at the real size of the device, and streams every action smoothly.

  • Vol+ / Vol-: volume up and down
  • Screenshot: capture the device screen
  • Power: turn the screen off
  • Rotate: rotate the screen
  • Install APK: install an application straight from the stream
  • ADB Command: run additional ADB commands
  • Reboot: restart the device
  • Switch Keyboard Method: open the keyboard settings
  • Accessibility Settings: open the settings interface
  • Language: open the language settings
  • Connection Setting: open connection settings such as Wi-Fi and Bluetooth
  • Apps: show the applications installed on the device

Group Control

Group Control synchronizes the devices: you work on one device and the rest repeat the same actions.

Select the devices you want to synchronize.

Then open the stream of any one of them to make it the main device.

Work on that device and the others follow identically.

Group Control is free and has no limit on the number of actions. The step-by-step version of this, together with installing an app on every phone, is on Synchronized control and APK installation.

Inspector

Inspector retrieves package and element information from an application, for use when writing automation code. It opens as a separate window from the GenFarmer app.

Inspector toolbar

  • Select Devices: the dropdown listing every device currently connected to GenFarmer
  • Reload: reload the current screen of the device
  • Enable Grid: show the element grid to identify elements precisely
  • Enable Swipe: swipe the phone directly on the Inspector screen
  • Enable Crop: capture an image and convert it to Base64 for the Image search node

What the Inspector view shows

Hover the mouse over the device interface to reveal the element blocks. Click an element and the middle column fills with information about that element and the application.

The right-hand column of the stream view shows:

  • Activity: the action of the element
  • XPathLite: the XPath used for scripting
  • ClassName: the class name of the element
  • Mouse Pos: coordinates of the selected position
  • Click: click the element and reload the device screen automatically
  • Element Rect: coordinates of the element
  • Index: the priority order of the element
  • Text: the text the element contains
  • Resourceid: the ID of the element
  • Package: the package ID of the application
  • Description: the description of the element

You can also select elements through the code view of the application.

Last updated 09/2026

Software

Account Manager

The account sheet inside the app: importing accounts, assigning devices, and choosing which account runs which script.

Account Manager keeps your account sheet inside the GenFarmer app, so you no longer need Excel or Google Sheets. In the roadmap you first use it on Day 2, and from Day 3 it is also where you choose which account runs which script.

Adding accounts

Add Accounts lets you add accounts by hand.

  • Please Input: the UID or username of the account
  • Select Format: a ready-made template per account type, or a custom one
  • Custom Format: choose the fields yourself - UID, Password, Email, 2FA and so on

Import and export

  • Export: export the account file in XLSX format
  • Import: import a CSV or XLSX file. GenFarmer ships a template file to work from

The columns

  • Device: the default device for that account
  • Status: the status of the account
  • UID: the username
  • Email: the email tied to the account
  • Password: the password
  • Email Password: the email password, used to read verification codes
  • 2FA: the authentication code

Once an account has been added, it runs on the device you selected, through whichever Module or App you use.

Assigning a device to an account is the step people skip. Copy the Device ID from the main screen into the Device ID column, otherwise the script has no phone to run on.

Software

Devices

Which devices your account may use, which are active, and how many slots are left.

The Devices screen manages the devices attached to your account.

Device overview

  • Total: the number of devices the account may use
  • Active: the devices currently in use
  • Free Slot: how many more can be added

Assigned and Unassigned

  • Assigned: devices that are active and ready to use
  • Unassigned: devices waiting to be activated. Click Active on the one you want and its status changes to Assigned

If a device refuses to become valid here, the cause is usually one of the two licence problems described in Fix: Not valid device.

Software

App Manager

The library of applications you push to the devices, from an APK file or from the in-app Store.

App Manager is the library of applications you can push to the devices.

  • Name: the name of the installed applications
  • Android Version: the minimum and target configuration
  • Size: the size of the APK file
  • Create At: when the file was added to GenFarmer
  • Remove: delete the APK file from GenFarmer
  • LoadAPK: upload an APK file into GenFarmer
How to change app with GenFarmer: install apps via APK or Store

Installing from the Store

Besides APK files, you can install from the Store, which carries applications GenFarmer has checked for safety and compatibility with most devices.

Download, then select version is the whole flow.

Choose the version that matches the Android version of the device, or the installation will throw errors. The device-side steps are on Synchronized control and APK installation.

PreviousDevices NextAutomation

Last updated 09/2026

Software

Automation

Apps, Modules, Tasks and Runs: how a script is built, stored, assigned to devices and tracked.

Automation is how the repetitive work moves from your hands to the machine, with the Antidetect features keeping the accounts alive. Whatever you run, there is a script for it: written by you, imported, or bought.

The day you start using it in the roadmap is Day 3, and the ready-made packages (Auto Login, Trust, Boost) are described there.

My Apps

  1. My App: the automations belonging to the account - written by you, imported, or bought from the store
  2. Subscription: the automations bought from the store, active and expired
  3. Import App: install an automation from a genfarmer file. Buy from the store or another source you trust, to avoid losing information
  4. Create App: create and write a new script
  • Select Devices: the device you want to test on
  • Select Node: the nodes that make no-code scripting possible
  • View Edit: shows the script and the nodes it uses
  • Top Bar: the supporting toolbars
  • Custom View: a custom cover image for the app
  • Device View: the device to stream
  • Table Column: design the data table for the information the script needs
  • Save: save the app and its nodes
  • Run: test the app on the selected device
  • Other functions: run logs, save as a different name, debug mode

My Modules

A Module is a more advanced form of an App, able to hold several smaller functions inside one Module. The interface and the functions are the same as Apps, with extra nodes for running several Apps inside the Module.

What sets a Module apart is the Context Menu node, which divides the functions and manages several Apps inside a single Module.

Saved Tasks

Saved Tasks holds the tasks you have already built, so running them again takes one click.

  • Name: the name of the task
  • App: the script app the task uses
  • Devices: how many devices run in the task
  • Runs: how many times it has run
  • Last run started at / Last run status / Last run duration: the most recent run

To create a task, go into a module or an app and choose Create Task.

Name the task, then pick the devices it should run on.

When you are done you can save and run it straight away, or save it for later.

Runs

Runs is where several tasks are managed at the same time.

  • App: the name of your script app
  • Task: the name of the task
  • Result: the number of runs
  • Started at / Finished at / Duration: when it ran and for how long
  • Status: Running, Done or Aborted
  • Run: run the task again

Tasks are created in Saved Tasks, and appear here once they are running.

Software

Settings

Language, storage location and the automation switches such as debug mode.

Settings holds the basic configuration of the software.

General

Choose the language, and light or dark mode.

Storage

  • Clear Cache: remove cached data and reset storage to its initial state
  • Storage Location: where files are kept on the computer. Left blank, they go to the GenFarmer directory
  • Use Selected Path: use the path you picked
  • Copy Profiles to Selected Path: copy the files into that path
  • Move Profiles to Selected Path: move the files into that path

Automation

  • Enable Debug Mode: turn on debugging
  • Tracking Node When Running: log the nodes while a module runs
  • Sync Profiles Data After Running Task: synchronize profile data after a task finishes

Reference

Fix: Not valid device

The app refuses the devices with a licence error. Two causes, one of which is simply a wrong clock.

The invalid device error in the GenFarmer app. Symptom: the app refuses the devices and shows a licence error.

Why it happens

  • You are signed in to the same GenFarmer account on more than one computer at the same time
  • You signed in on one computer and then signed in on another
  • The date, time or time zone on your computer does not match the real time

A GenFarmer account supports one email signed in on one computer at a time.

How to fix it

  1. Sign out everywhere

    Sign out of the GenFarmer account on every computer.

  2. Check the clock

    Check that the date, time and time zone match the real time in your region. If they do not, open Date and Time Settings, click Sync now, then check the time zone again. If your clock was already correct, go to step 3.

  3. Sign in on one computer only

    If the licence error is still there, select and delete every device shown in the control panel, close the application and open it again.

If you were never signed in anywhere else, do step 2 anyway - a wrong clock alone causes this error. If it persists, contact support.

PreviousTroubleshoot by symptom NextFix: Device not showing (Unauthorized)

Last updated 09/2026

Reference

Fix: Device not showing (Unauthorized)

Some phones never appear on screen because ADB reports them as unauthorized. Fixed with the ADB key file for your box model.

This is the main reason some phones in the box never show up on screen. The devices are connected, but ADB reports them as unauthorized, so GenFarmer cannot use them.

How to fix it

  1. Open Command Prompt

    Press Windows + R, type cmd and press Enter.

  2. Check the device status

    Type adb devices. Every device listed as unauthorized has this problem.

  3. Ask support for the ADB Key file

    The key depends on the Boxphone model you own, so contact GenFarmer support to get the right file. See Before you contact support for what to send them.

  4. Put the key in the right folder

    Go to C: drive → Users → your computer name → .android

  5. Replace the existing key

    Copy the ADB Key file from GenFarmer into that folder, choosing Replace if prompted.

  6. Restart the ADB server

    Back in Command Prompt, run the two commands below. When every device shows the status device, the problem is fixed.

adb kill-server
adb devices

If adb devices recognises nothing at all, ADB is not installed on your computer yet. Set up the ADB environment first - see ADB commands and the requirements on Requirements and safety notes.

PreviousFix: Not valid device NextFix: Boxphone indicator light error

Last updated 09/2026

Reference

Fix: Boxphone indicator light error

A red or flickering light is a hardware fault on one mainboard. How to tell the slot from the board.

Symptoms: the indicator light flickers or shows red, and the device drops out of the software.

The normal light code is blue for USB mode and green for OTG mode. Red or flickering is a hardware fault on that mainboard, not a software problem.

How to fix it

  1. Swap the mainboard to another slot

    Remove the mainboards that are misbehaving and swap their positions with others. This tells you whether the fault follows the slot or the mainboard.

  2. Reseat the FPC cable

    If the problem persists after the swap, adjust or reseat the FPC cable - the long orange ribbon running along the mainboard.

Reseating the FPC cable means opening the box. If you are not comfortable doing it, contact support first: hardware altered without guidance falls outside the warranty, as noted on Requirements and safety notes.

Reference

FAQ

The questions customers ask most often, with links to the page that answers each one in full.

The questions customers ask most often. Where a full page already answers one, this page links to it instead of repeating it.

Hardware and setup

What do I need to run a Boxphone system?

  • Hardware: a Boxphone, and optionally a router for IP security and network management
  • Software: the automation tools and scripts
  • Resources: an internet connection, social media accounts, and proxies or a VPN to give the devices different IP addresses

The full requirement list, including PC specification and voltage, is on Requirements and safety notes.

How can each device in a Boxphone have a different IP address?

Use a proxy or VPN per device. GenFarmer also sells GenRouter, which assigns the proxy or VPN quickly, spoofs network parameters such as MAC, DNS and WebRTC, and blocks LAN scanning. Why this matters for account survival is explained on Day 2, and everything about choosing the device and the proxies is on Router & proxy.

How many accounts can I run on one Boxphone?

  • Safest: 1 account per platform per device
  • You can push further, but the recommended maximum is 3 accounts per platform per device

Can I rent a Boxphone to try it first?

You can rent Cloudphone services from GenFarmer to test the system: Cloudphone GenFarmer for social media use, and Genplay.io for gaming or idle gaming.

Does it work with emulators as well as boxes?

Yes. GenFarmer describes itself as covering screen streaming and no-code automation for phone farms and emulators. There is a walkthrough for LDPlayer on the channel: How to connect LDPlayer with GenFarmer - automation on emulator.

What it can and cannot do

Can a Boxphone be used for gaming or idle gaming?

Yes, particularly for lightweight or idle games and simple automation such as auto check-in, pet raising or farming systems. Supported examples include Roblox, Play Together and Clash of Clans. Heavy games such as Lien Quan, Ymir or Raven2 need a Boxphone model with matching hardware.

Does GenFarmer teach how to make money with a Boxphone?

No. GenFarmer documents how to use the devices and software in its own ecosystem - operating, installing, optimizing and managing devices - so you can apply those tools to your own work.

Where do I start learning no-code automation?

GenFarmer runs free no-code automation courses, from basic to advanced, on the GenFarmer Vietnam YouTube channel. The features those courses use are described on Automation.

Router & proxy

Choosing between the H3000 and a mini PC, what the network has to provide, preparing proxies, and what to do when the network drops.

Everything about the network layer in one place. Until now this was spread over three pages: one line in The four layers, the wiring table in Day 0, and step 2 of Day 2.

The control panel itself - assigning a proxy to each phone and switching on Isolate Mode - is shown in the video below and in Day 2 · step 2. This page does not repeat it; it covers what you decide before you open that panel.

How to use GenRouter: assign a proxy per device

1. H3000 or mini PC

You runDeviceConnection modeWhy
1 box (20 phones)GenRouter H3000USBThe H3000 is stable up to 30 connected devices. One box of 20 phones fits; two boxes is 40 and does not
2 boxes or moreMini PCOTGThe mini PC handles up to 200 devices, which is exactly 10 boxes, and the phones use its network
1 box, but you already own a mini PCMini PCOTGWorks the same on a single box
Neither device-OTG or Wi-FiFlexible, depending on your needs

This one choice decides the rest of Day 1: the H3000 goes with Connect via USB, the mini PC with Connect via OTG (LAN). Picking the wrong row is the single most common reason Scan finds no phones.

Pushing an H3000 past 30 devices can crash it or disable some of its functions. That is the hard number behind the rule that one H3000 cannot drive two boxes.

GenRouter H3000Mini PC
Network ports1 x LAN 2.5Gbps + 1 x WAN 1Gbps4 x Intel i226 2.5G
Devices it can hold30 (stable limit)200
Built-in Wi-FiWi-Fi 6, dual band, and it can broadcast 32 separate networksNone - needs a separate access point
Runs off a power bankYesNo
Control panel192.168.5.1:9000192.168.5.1:9000

2. What the network has to provide

You runDeviceHow the ports are usedSwitch
1 box - 20 phonesGenRouter H3000LAN cable from the modem into the WAN port; your PC into the LAN port, or onto the GENROUTER-2.4GHz / GENROUTER-5GHz Wi-FiNot needed
2 to 4 boxes - 40 to 80 phonesMini PCModem into ETH0; your PC into ETH1, ETH2 or ETH3; the boxes into the remaining portsOnly if you run out of ports
Up to 10 boxes - 200 phonesMini PC + switchModem into ETH0, everything else through the switchYes. The documented stable cluster is 1 PC + 1 mini PC + 1 switch + 10 boxes
More than 10 boxesA second clusterRepeat the cluster aboveYes, one per cluster

GenFarmer's own reference architecture is that cluster of 1 control PC, 1 mini PC, 1 switch and 10 boxes. A switch is recommended from the point where you are running a large number of boxes, both for stability and to spread the load off the mini PC.

  • One free port on the modem for the GenRouter
  • LAN cable: Cat5 or Cat6, Cat6 preferred, to keep transmission stable
  • The control computer does not need Wi-Fi - LAN works just as well, for both the H3000 and the mini PC
  • Minimum bandwidth: around 100 Mbps for the first box and about 100 Mbps more for each box after it. TikTok is the heaviest platform, so that is the figure to plan by. Keep it to a maximum of 5 boxes on one internet line.

In practice the proxy is the bottleneck, not your home line. If the line measures fine and the farm still stutters, suspect the proxies before you pay for more bandwidth.

If you need Wi-Fi and you are on a mini PC

The mini PC connects by LAN only and cannot broadcast Wi-Fi, so USB Boxphones and ordinary phones need a separate device for that. Use an access point - Aruba, Cisco and TP-Link all make them - set to Access Point mode so it only broadcasts, while the router keeps control and hands out the IPs. Ask the seller for a model that can handle your number of connections.

3. How it connects to the rest of the system

You useHow to connect
GenRouter H3000Power first: the router lights up green. Modem to WAN, PC to LAN. No LAN port on the PC? Join the GENROUTER-2.4GHz or GENROUTER-5GHz network instead. Then open 192.168.5.1:9000; if the dashboard appears, you are connected.
GenRouter H3000 without a LAN cableOn V3 the router can pick up another Wi-Fi network and rebroadcast it. Join GENROUTER-2.4GHz or GENROUTER-5GHz, choose the network to rebroadcast, enter its password, submit.
Mini PCPower first. Modem into ETH0, PC into ETH1/ETH2/ETH3, the boxes into the remaining ports, then open 192.168.5.1:9000.
Box to box over LANBoxphones that have a LAN port can be chained to each other and run in OTG mode. If the devices are not detected, connect over USB first so they are picked up, then switch back to OTG.
You useHow to connect the control computer
GenRouter H3000If your PC has Wi-Fi, connect over Wi-Fi. If not, use LAN: H3000 LAN port to PC Ethernet, or Boxphone LAN to PC Ethernet (in this case connect the H3000 LAN port to LAN1 or LAN2 of the Boxphone, then run the remaining LAN port to the PC - this applies to the P30 box).
Mini PCConnect over LAN. Wi-Fi does not apply. If your laptop has no Ethernet port, get a USB-to-LAN or Type-C-to-LAN adapter.

Never press the physical reset button on the router unless technical support tells you to, and do not run ipconfig or change the device IP on your computer by hand without guidance. Both are quick ways to lose a working setup.

Put the router in the middle of the room, or as close to the devices as you can. 2.4GHz reaches further but is slower; 5GHz is faster but shorter range. If you replace the charger or cable, use an Android phone charger with a Type-C cable - the router is picky about cables, and an older phone's cable often works when a new one does not.

Once it is wired, the software side is on Connect via USB or Connect via OTG (LAN).

4. Preparing proxies

An account is an identity. A proxy is that identity's home address. If 20 accounts share one address, the platform sees 20 people living in the same house, waking and sleeping at the same time, liking the same kind of content - which is why accounts get banned in clusters rather than one by one.

What follows from that:

  • One proxy per phone. A full box of 20 phones needs 20 proxies
  • For a trial run, 5 accounts over 2-3 proxies is acceptable, but that is not a production setup
  • Isolate Mode is the safety net: when a proxy dies, expires or errors, that phone is cut off from the internet immediately instead of falling back to your real address
  • Besides assigning the proxy, GenRouter also hides WebRTC, DNS, MAC address, location and timezone, blocks traceroute and stops LAN scanning

Which proxies the router accepts

TypeFormat and notes
SOCKS5socks5://user:pass@ip:port
HTTP / HTTPSSupported, same workflow
PPPoEBuilds proxies from a multi-session line. Vietnam only - the number of proxies equals the number of sessions on the line, and the IPs come from a real ISP
DPNGenRouter's own network: residential proxies from 10 different countries, and you can rotate to another country when that country has enough shared supply

Adding them

  1. Prepare the list first, in Notepad or a document, one proxy per line
  2. Open 192.168.5.1:9000, go to Proxy Manager, click Add proxy
  3. Pick the format that matches your list, paste it in, click Add proxy
  4. To assign: Devices → Update proxy, choose the protocol, then either type the details (Input Manually) or pick one already stored (Select from List)
  5. To do the whole box at once, tick the checkbox at the top of the device table, click Update Proxy and paste the list - the router hands a different proxy to each device

Buying advice - which provider, residential against datacenter, and whether rotating proxies are safe on a farm - is the one part still open. Ask support rather than guessing. If you are in Vietnam, the multi-session PPPoE route means you may not need to buy proxies at all.

5. When something goes wrong

What you seeUsual causeWhat to do
Phones show up but cannot get onlineProxy is dead, or the time on the phone is wrongCheck the proxy first; if the proxy is fine, correct the phone's clock to match your PC
Accounts get banned in clustersSeveral accounts sharing one addressOne proxy per phone, then start again with fresh accounts - see Day 2
The whole box loses internet at onceWiring or the shared uplink, not the proxiesCheck the LAN run and the free port on the modem, then re-Scan
One phone drops off the network on its ownIsolate Mode cutting that phone because its proxy diedReplace that proxy; this is the feature working, not a fault
Connection is unstable once you add more boxesThe mini PC is carrying every box directlyAdd a LAN switch between the mini PC and the boxes
Devices are not detected in OTG modeThey were never picked up in the first placeConnect over USB so they register, then switch back to OTG
Scan finds no phonesBoth address ranges not added, or Activate never clickedConnect via OTG (LAN) - on USB, check the light is blue
The indicator light is red or flickeringMainboard or FPC cable, not the networkFix: indicator light error

More symptoms, including the software-side ones, are in Troubleshoot by symptom.

Where to go next

  • Watch the walkthrough above to see the panel being used
  • If you are following the course, the network work happens on Day 2 - Mastering identity
  • The per-phone steps - assign proxy, switch on Isolate Mode - are in Day 2 · step 2

Reference

Automation packages

What a package is, which ones exist, the three tools inside them and the order you have to keep.

A package is a ready-made set of automation scripts for one platform. You buy it from the Store inside the app; from then on it sits in Automation → My Apps and runs like any other app. If you have not bought one yet, contact GenFarmer sales on WhatsApp +84 97 123 46 01 or ask in the customer community. Everything here can also be done by hand, just far more slowly.

Packages available today

PlatformPackageWhat it doesDetail
TikTokTrustWarms the account up around the keywords of your nicheTikTok
BoostViews, likes, comments and shares on a video you choose
FacebookTrustWarms the account up on one content nicheFacebook
BoostLikes, shares and comments on a post or a Reel
InstagramTrustWarms the account up, posts at a basic levelInstagram
BoostLikes, comments, views and saves, aimed at non-followers
SpotifySpotifyValid streams for your tracksSpotify
TidalTidalValid streams for your tracksTidal

Packages are updated separately from this documentation. If a package behaves differently from what you read here, the package is the newer one - tell support so this page can be corrected.

Why a phone farm rather than an antidetect browser

Social and music platforms have stopped being workable from an antidetect browser: a browser leaks its pattern, checkpoints come fast, engagement gets suppressed, and a browser cannot produce a listening environment that counts as a real stream. Real devices on real IPs are what the packages are built for.

The four steps every package follows

Warm the account up → Set its audience → Trigger engagement → Push distribution

Nothing gets pushed on day one, and nothing is spammed. The whole sequence is timed the way a real person behaves.

What the algorithms actually measure

The same five signals decide reach on TikTok, Instagram and Facebook, which is why the packages are shaped the way they are.

SignalWhat it means for you
Watch time and retentionThe most important one: did the viewer stop, watch to the end, rewatch, or leave early
Early engagementLikes, shares, comments and saves have to appear soon after posting. Dumped all at once looks wrong and gets suppressed; spread out naturally, the data flows in evenly
Who is engagingNot just how many, but whether they match the content. The wrong audience teaches the algorithm the wrong thing and the distribution goes to the wrong people
The account's historyAn account that has run junk engagement is very hard to recover. Starting correctly matters more than running hard
Non-follower responseThe platform is asking whether new people react, not whether your followers do. A post that wakes up non-followers spreads fast even from a small account

The three tools, and the order you must keep

TrustBoost
GoalWarm the account up, build its historyAmplify a piece of content
FocusStability and safetyPerformance and growth
SuitsNew accounts, or accounts being rebuiltAccounts that already have a base
BehaviourWatches by topic, follows and likes selectivelyDrives views, likes, streams, trending

Auto Login sits in front of both: it logs the accounts in and out in bulk instead of you typing on each phone.

Turning on Boost for an account that has not been warmed up is the fastest way to lose it, and an account that has once run junk engagement is very hard to recover. This is the most expensive mistake in the whole roadmap.

The step-by-step version of running these is on Day 3 - Basic automation.

What keeps it looking human

  • Randomised tasks: the timing and the type of action - view, like, save, scroll - change from run to run instead of repeating a fixed pattern
  • Limits and rest: the scripts have ceilings, pauses and background behaviour (watch, scroll, skip past), which is what the platform reads as natural
  • Built per platform: each package is shaped around how that platform distributes content and how its users behave in your market

Before you run your first package

  • The 5 trial accounts have survived the 48-hour hands-off period without a verification prompt - see Day 2
  • Every phone has its own proxy, and Isolate Mode is on - see Router & proxy
  • The platform app is installed on every phone, from the official APK folder rather than the links in the User Manual - see Day 2 · step 3
  • You have a vilao.ai API key if the package comments automatically - this was part of Day 0, and finding out halfway through that you do not have one means stopping halfway

Where to go next

  • The procedure for downloading and running a package is on Day 3 - Basic automation
  • The Store itself is described in App Manager; Apps, Tasks and Runs in Automation

Reference

TikTok package

Trust and Boost for TikTok: what is in the package, the figures to run it by, and what to watch for.

What is in the package

TikTok has both halves of the set. Trust builds an account with a real activity history so it stops being treated as a throwaway; Boost runs the publishing side across the accounts you own.

Trust - growing the account

  • Grow by industry keywords. The system searches out videos around your target keywords and behaves like a real person browsing them, so the account starts being recommended into those keywords and builds a history that makes it look trustworthy.
  • Auto-post and comment with AI. Posting runs on a schedule, and the comments are generated rather than copied, so a large number of accounts never end up writing the same line.
  • Manage many accounts at once. Hundreds of accounts on one screen, synchronised and monitored centrally, with no technical skill required.
  • Random follows. The system picks from the suggested list and follows at random, so the behaviour never looks like a repeated action.

Boost - running the channels

  • Contextual AI replies. AI reads the comments left on your own videos and drafts replies that match the topic and the tone, phrased differently every time.
  • Schedule and publish at scale. Upload and schedule videos in bulk across your account list, publish at the right time for each market, and stagger the publishing so channels are not posting identically.
  • One interface for every account, with per-channel reporting instead of numbers scattered across logins.

Because it all runs on real handsets, the uploads happen in a native mobile environment rather than a browser.

Operating figures

The last row is still waiting on the technical team. The rest are the recommended settings, not hard limits.

QuestionAnswer
How long to warm up before running for real2-4 weeks of Trust
How many accounts per device1 per platform per device is safest; 3 is the recommended maximum
Maximum phones on one router30 on a GenRouter H3000, 200 on a mini PC - see Router & proxy
Maximum boxes on one internet line5, at roughly 100 Mbps per box - see Router & proxy
How often to change the information tied to the linkevery 7 to 14 days
How long to run before resting the accounts4-6 hours on, 1-2 hours off, or several separate windows through the day. Never 24/7 - no real person scrolls all night
Suggested settings for a beginnerTo be confirmed

Why change the information tied to the link every week or two. Change it every day and nobody keeps up; leave it untouched for months and you are hammering the same list of links over and over, which is exactly the pattern the platforms sweep for.

Notes specific to TikTok

  • TikTok is the heaviest platform for bandwidth, which is why the 100 Mbps per box figure is based on it.
  • The APK links for TikTok in the User Manual are the only ones that are correct, but the official folder in Day 2 · step 3 is still the safer source.
  • Automatic commenting needs a vilao.ai API key - get it during Day 0, not on the day you need it.

The TikTok chapter of the User Manual is p. 17-50, 34 pages - the most complete chapter in the manual.

Signs that mean stop

  1. Accounts are asked for verification more often than usual.
  2. Posts suddenly lose all their views.
  3. The number of running phones keeps dropping for no clear reason.

If any account is banned overnight, do not scale up. Go back and check the proxies and the running speed first.

Facebook package

Trust and Boost for Facebook: what is in the package, the figures to run it by, and what to watch for.

What is in the package

Facebook is the platform with the most ground covered: Trust rebuilds the account itself, Boost does the seeding.

Trust - growing the account

  • Keeps the profile looking lived-in. Profile picture and cover, bio, interests and occupation, the friend list and auto-adding friends, even the location and activity region - all updated automatically, because an empty profile is what gets checkpointed.
  • AI-powered posting. Posts on a schedule with varied, natural content, shares from pages and groups to generate interaction, mixes content formats, and follows patterns taken from how real users actually post rather than a fixed loop.

Boost - seeding and reach

  • AI auto-seeding for pages and profiles. Reactions and comments on the posts you target, written by AI from the context, with several accounts creating the community effect, and the wording varied so nothing repeats.
  • Auto-join groups by keyword. Finds groups in your field, joins them with secondary accounts, and posts related content into them.
  • Livestream engagement. Raises viewers and reactions during a live, sends hearts, comments in context, and reshares the stream to profiles and groups.
  • Content spinning. Dozens of variants from one base text, so Facebook does not read the posts as spam or duplicate.
  • Auto-sharing to profiles and groups for wider reach.

Why change the information tied to the link every week or two. Change it every day and nobody keeps up; leave it untouched for months and you are hammering the same list of links over and over, which is exactly the pattern the platforms sweep for.

Notes specific to Facebook

  • Facebook is the platform where accounts are asked to verify soonest, so log in by hand first and watch what happens before handing it to a script - Day 2.
  • Automatic commenting needs a vilao.ai API key.

The Facebook chapter of the User Manual is p. 51-77, 27 pages.

Do not download the Facebook APK from the links in the User Manual. Those links currently point to the TikTok file. Use the official folder listed in Day 2 · step 3.

Signs that mean stop

  1. Accounts are asked for verification more often than usual.
  2. Posts suddenly lose all their views.
  3. The number of running phones keeps dropping for no clear reason.

If any account is banned overnight, do not scale up. Go back and check the proxies and the running speed first.

Reference

Instagram package

Trust and Boost for Instagram: what is in the package, the figures to run it by, and what to watch for.

What is in the package

Trust grows the account on real devices; Boost handles publishing and community management across the accounts you own.

Trust - growing the account

  • Login, logout and account management. Many accounts at once, each with its status visible, so the manual slips disappear.
  • Keyword search and AI nurturing. The system looks up content and hashtags around your keywords, the account interacts with what it finds the way a real user would, and it follows at random by keyword or by link.
  • Randomised actions. Browsing the feed, watching reels, liking and commenting on relevant content, with the mix deliberately randomised. The like, comment and view ratios are adjustable per growth phase, and the behaviour is spread across the day to keep checkpoints away.
  • AI posting. Images and captions generated from the topic you choose, which keeps the account active and consistent.

Boost - running the accounts

  • Posts, reels and stories from images you already have, with AI writing the captions, in bulk, on a steady daily schedule.
  • Community management on your own posts. Comments from every account in one place, with AI drafting replies to genuine questions for you to approve, and resharing your posts to stories.
  • Reels at scale. Bulk upload and scheduling, captions and hashtags per market, publishing at the right local time.
  • Profile upkeep. Bios, links and profile details kept consistent across brand accounts, avatars and highlight covers updated in bulk, login health visible on one dashboard, and reel performance tracked across all accounts.

Operating figures

The last row is still waiting on the technical team. The rest are the recommended settings, not hard limits.

QuestionAnswer
How long to warm up before running for real2-4 weeks of Trust
How many accounts per device1 per platform per device is safest; 3 is the recommended maximum
Maximum phones on one router30 on a GenRouter H3000, 200 on a mini PC - see Router & proxy
Maximum boxes on one internet line5, at roughly 100 Mbps per box - see Router & proxy
How often to change the information tied to the linkevery 7 to 14 days
How long to run before resting the accounts4-6 hours on, 1-2 hours off, or several separate windows through the day. Never 24/7 - no real person scrolls all night
Suggested settings for a beginnerTo be confirmed

Why change the information tied to the link every week or two. Change it every day and nobody keeps up; leave it untouched for months and you are hammering the same list of links over and over, which is exactly the pattern the platforms sweep for.

Notes specific to Instagram

  • Instagram remembers an account's history. One that has run junk engagement is very hard to recover, while one warmed up properly from the start gets noticeably more reach on exactly the same content. Trust before Boost is not optional here.
  • Trust includes posting, so decide your content niche before you start - a wrongly set niche means the account is warmed up as somebody interested in something else.
  • Automatic commenting needs a vilao.ai API key.

The Instagram chapter of the User Manual is p. 78-101, 24 pages.

Do not download the Instagram APK from the links in the User Manual. Those links currently point to the TikTok file. Use the official folder listed in Day 2 · step 3.

  1. Accounts are asked for verification more often than usual.
  2. Posts suddenly lose all their views.
  3. The number of running phones keeps dropping for no clear reason.

If any account is banned overnight, do not scale up. Go back and check the proxies and the running speed first.
